{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+C\+A\+P\+\_\+\+A\+D\+D\+F\+B2\+\_\+\+M\+O\+D\+I\+F\+I\+E\+RS~0x10}

D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+C\+A\+P\+\_\+\+A\+D\+D\+F\+B2\+\_\+\+M\+O\+D\+I\+F\+I\+E\+RS

If set to 1, the driver supports supplying modifiers in the \&D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+I\+O\+C\+T\+L\+\_\+\+M\+O\+D\+E\+\_\+\+A\+D\+D\+F\+B2 ioctl. \mbox{\Hypertarget{drm_8h_a6676b2a20f5f8d3018d259cba0061047}\label{drm_8h_a6676b2a20f5f8d3018d259cba0061047}}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+C\+A\+P\+\_\+\+A\+S\+Y\+N\+C\+\_\+\+P\+A\+G\+E\+\_\+\+F\+L\+IP~0x7}

D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+C\+A\+P\+\_\+\+A\+S\+Y\+N\+C\+\_\+\+P\+A\+G\+E\+\_\+\+F\+L\+IP

If set to 1, the driver supports \&D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+M\+O\+D\+E\+\_\+\+P\+A\+G\+E\+\_\+\+F\+L\+I\+P\+\_\+\+A\+S\+Y\+NC. \mbox{\Hypertarget{drm_8h_aa47920a5a009a1d574d22c4c974b4ddb}\label{drm_8h_aa47920a5a009a1d574d22c4c974b4ddb}} 
\index{drm.h@{drm.h}!DRM\_CAP\_CRTC\_IN\_VBLANK\_EVENT@{DRM\_CAP\_CRTC\_IN\_VBLANK\_EVENT}}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+C\+A\+P\+\_\+\+C\+R\+T\+C\+\_\+\+I\+N\+\_\+\+V\+B\+L\+A\+N\+K\+\_\+\+E\+V\+E\+NT~0x12}

D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+C\+A\+P\+\_\+\+C\+R\+T\+C\+\_\+\+I\+N\+\_\+\+V\+B\+L\+A\+N\+K\+\_\+\+E\+V\+E\+NT

If set to 1, the kernel supports reporting the C\+R\+TC ID in \&\mbox{\hyperlink{structdrm__event__vblank_aa79f0cb1f3d404c5a0d89caa1282a206}{drm\+\_\+event\+\_\+vblank.\+crtc\+\_\+id}} for the \&D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+E\+V\+E\+N\+T\+\_\+\+V\+B\+L\+A\+NK and \&D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+E\+V\+E\+N\+T\+\_\+\+F\+L\+I\+P\+\_\+\+C\+O\+M\+P\+L\+E\+TE events.

Starting kernel version 4.\+12, this capability is always set to 1. \mbox{\Hypertarget{drm_8h_af2388844845674bfb5f30c2daba5b7ec}\label{drm_8h_af2388844845674bfb5f30c2daba5b7ec}} 
\index{drm.h@{drm.h}!DRM\_CAP\_CURSOR\_HEIGHT@{DRM\_CAP\_CURSOR\_HEIGHT}}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+C\+A\+P\+\_\+\+C\+U\+R\+S\+O\+R\+\_\+\+H\+E\+I\+G\+HT~0x9}

D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+C\+A\+P\+\_\+\+C\+U\+R\+S\+O\+R\+\_\+\+H\+E\+I\+G\+HT

See \&D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+C\+A\+P\+\_\+\+C\+U\+R\+S\+O\+R\+\_\+\+W\+I\+D\+TH. \mbox{\Hypertarget{drm_8h_afafeed5861d0fc1dc956b8bf4b447582}\label{drm_8h_afafeed5861d0fc1dc956b8bf4b447582}} 
\index{drm.h@{drm.h}!DRM\_CAP\_CURSOR\_WIDTH@{DRM\_CAP\_CURSOR\_WIDTH}}
\index{DRM\_CAP\_CURSOR\_WIDTH@{DRM\_CAP\_CURSOR\_WIDTH}!drm.h@{drm.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{DRM\_CAP\_CURSOR\_WIDTH}{DRM\_CAP\_CURSOR\_WIDTH}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+C\+A\+P\+\_\+\+C\+U\+R\+S\+O\+R\+\_\+\+W\+I\+D\+TH~0x8}

D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+C\+A\+P\+\_\+\+C\+U\+R\+S\+O\+R\+\_\+\+W\+I\+D\+TH

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} {\ttfamily C\+U\+R\+S\+O\+R\+\_\+\+W\+I\+D\+TH} and {\ttfamily C\+U\+R\+S\+O\+R\+\_\+\+H\+E\+I\+G\+HT} capabilities return a valid width x height combination for the hardware cursor. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} intention is that a hardware agnostic userspace can query a cursor plane size to use.

Note that the cross-\/driver contract is to merely return a valid size; drivers are free to attach another meaning on top, eg. i915 returns the maximum plane size. \mbox{\Hypertarget{drm_8h_a67125931997092d387b6f49e7b013101}\label{drm_8h_a67125931997092d387b6f49e7b013101}} 
\index{drm.h@{drm.h}!DRM\_CAP\_DUMB\_BUFFER@{DRM\_CAP\_DUMB\_BUFFER}}
\index{DRM\_CAP\_DUMB\_BUFFER@{DRM\_CAP\_DUMB\_BUFFER}!drm.h@{drm.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{DRM\_CAP\_DUMB\_BUFFER}{DRM\_CAP\_DUMB\_BUFFER}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+C\+A\+P\+\_\+\+D\+U\+M\+B\+\_\+\+B\+U\+F\+F\+ER~0x1}

D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+C\+A\+P\+\_\+\+D\+U\+M\+B\+\_\+\+B\+U\+F\+F\+ER

If set to 1, the driver supports creating dumb buffers via the \&D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+I\+O\+C\+T\+L\+\_\+\+M\+O\+D\+E\+\_\+\+C\+R\+E\+A\+T\+E\+\_\+\+D\+U\+MB ioctl. \mbox{\Hypertarget{drm_8h_acad5b6cce18d53335d76705556cc78d8}\label{drm_8h_acad5b6cce18d53335d76705556cc78d8}} 
\index{drm.h@{drm.h}!DRM\_CAP\_DUMB\_PREFER\_SHADOW@{DRM\_CAP\_DUMB\_PREFER\_SHADOW}}
\index{DRM\_CAP\_DUMB\_PREFER\_SHADOW@{DRM\_CAP\_DUMB\_PREFER\_SHADOW}!drm.h@{drm.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{DRM\_CAP\_DUMB\_PREFER\_SHADOW}{DRM\_CAP\_DUMB\_PREFER\_SHADOW}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+C\+A\+P\+\_\+\+D\+U\+M\+B\+\_\+\+P\+R\+E\+F\+E\+R\+\_\+\+S\+H\+A\+D\+OW~0x4}

D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+C\+A\+P\+\_\+\+D\+U\+M\+B\+\_\+\+P\+R\+E\+F\+E\+R\+\_\+\+S\+H\+A\+D\+OW

If set to 1, the driver prefers userspace to render to a shadow buffer instead of directly rendering to a dumb buffer. For best speed, userspace should do streaming ordered memory copies into the dumb buffer and never read from it.

Note that this preference only applies to dumb buffers, it\textquotesingle{}s irrelevant for other types of buffers. \mbox{\Hypertarget{drm_8h_a91ebe668f3e3b69838114dded6fcb20d}\label{drm_8h_a91ebe668f3e3b69838114dded6fcb20d}} 
\index{drm.h@{drm.h}!DRM\_CAP\_DUMB\_PREFERRED\_DEPTH@{DRM\_CAP\_DUMB\_PREFERRED\_DEPTH}}
\index{DRM\_CAP\_DUMB\_PREFERRED\_DEPTH@{DRM\_CAP\_DUMB\_PREFERRED\_DEPTH}!drm.h@{drm.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{DRM\_CAP\_DUMB\_PREFERRED\_DEPTH}{DRM\_CAP\_DUMB\_PREFERRED\_DEPTH}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+C\+A\+P\+\_\+\+D\+U\+M\+B\+\_\+\+P\+R\+E\+F\+E\+R\+R\+E\+D\+\_\+\+D\+E\+P\+TH~0x3}

D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+C\+A\+P\+\_\+\+D\+U\+M\+B\+\_\+\+P\+R\+E\+F\+E\+R\+R\+E\+D\+\_\+\+D\+E\+P\+TH

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} preferred bit depth for dumb buffers.

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} bit depth is the number of bits used to indicate the color of a single pixel excluding any padding. This is different from the number of bits per pixel. For instance, X\+R\+G\+B8888 has a bit depth of 24 but has 32 bits per pixel.

Note that this preference only applies to dumb buffers, it\textquotesingle{}s irrelevant for other types of buffers. \mbox{\Hypertarget{drm_8h_a9cc4f71e57649686e23444f63668471a}\label{drm_8h_a9cc4f71e57649686e23444f63668471a}} 
\index{drm.h@{drm.h}!DRM\_CAP\_PAGE\_FLIP\_TARGET@{DRM\_CAP\_PAGE\_FLIP\_TARGET}}
\index{DRM\_CAP\_PAGE\_FLIP\_TARGET@{DRM\_CAP\_PAGE\_FLIP\_TARGET}!drm.h@{drm.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{DRM\_CAP\_PAGE\_FLIP\_TARGET}{DRM\_CAP\_PAGE\_FLIP\_TARGET}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+C\+A\+P\+\_\+\+P\+A\+G\+E\+\_\+\+F\+L\+I\+P\+\_\+\+T\+A\+R\+G\+ET~0x11}

D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+C\+A\+P\+\_\+\+P\+A\+G\+E\+\_\+\+F\+L\+I\+P\+\_\+\+T\+A\+R\+G\+ET

If set to 1, the driver supports the \&D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+M\+O\+D\+E\+\_\+\+P\+A\+G\+E\+\_\+\+F\+L\+I\+P\+\_\+\+T\+A\+R\+G\+E\+T\+\_\+\+A\+B\+S\+O\+L\+U\+TE and \&D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+M\+O\+D\+E\+\_\+\+P\+A\+G\+E\+\_\+\+F\+L\+I\+P\+\_\+\+T\+A\+R\+G\+E\+T\+\_\+\+R\+E\+L\+A\+T\+I\+VE flags in \&\mbox{\hyperlink{structdrm__mode__crtc__page__flip__target_a757f23452480aff08e642555c29e4466}{drm\+\_\+mode\+\_\+crtc\+\_\+page\+\_\+flip\+\_\+target.\+flags}} for the \&D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+I\+O\+C\+T\+L\+\_\+\+M\+O\+D\+E\+\_\+\+P\+A\+G\+E\+\_\+\+F\+L\+IP ioctl. \mbox{\Hypertarget{drm_8h_aac9e837d2891238a0ff29f114f6ebc4b}\label{drm_8h_aac9e837d2891238a0ff29f114f6ebc4b}} 
\index{drm.h@{drm.h}!DRM\_CAP\_PRIME@{DRM\_CAP\_PRIME}}
\index{DRM\_CAP\_PRIME@{DRM\_CAP\_PRIME}!drm.h@{drm.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{DRM\_CAP\_PRIME}{DRM\_CAP\_PRIME}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+C\+A\+P\+\_\+\+P\+R\+I\+ME~0x5}

D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+C\+A\+P\+\_\+\+P\+R\+I\+ME

Bitfield of supported P\+R\+I\+ME sharing capabilities. See \&D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+P\+R\+I\+M\+E\+\_\+\+C\+A\+P\+\_\+\+I\+M\+P\+O\+RT and \&D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+P\+R\+I\+M\+E\+\_\+\+C\+A\+P\+\_\+\+E\+X\+P\+O\+RT.

P\+R\+I\+ME buffers are exposed as dma-\/buf file descriptors. See Documentation/gpu/drm-\/mm.\+rst, section \char`\"{}\+P\+R\+I\+M\+E Buffer Sharing\char`\"{}. \mbox{\Hypertarget{drm_8h_a28bcc277b16f98b0ea843ace92f4fa81}\label{drm_8h_a28bcc277b16f98b0ea843ace92f4fa81}} 
\index{drm.h@{drm.h}!DRM\_CAP\_SYNCOBJ@{DRM\_CAP\_SYNCOBJ}}
\index{DRM\_CAP\_SYNCOBJ@{DRM\_CAP\_SYNCOBJ}!drm.h@{drm.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{DRM\_CAP\_SYNCOBJ}{DRM\_CAP\_SYNCOBJ}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+C\+A\+P\+\_\+\+S\+Y\+N\+C\+O\+BJ~0x13}

D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+C\+A\+P\+\_\+\+S\+Y\+N\+C\+O\+BJ

If set to 1, the driver supports sync objects. See Documentation/gpu/drm-\/mm.\+rst, section \char`\"{}\+D\+R\+M Sync Objects\char`\"{}. \mbox{\Hypertarget{drm_8h_a786a0230f620ced1c110c34228576a12}\label{drm_8h_a786a0230f620ced1c110c34228576a12}} 
\index{drm.h@{drm.h}!DRM\_CAP\_SYNCOBJ\_TIMELINE@{DRM\_CAP\_SYNCOBJ\_TIMELINE}}
\index{DRM\_CAP\_SYNCOBJ\_TIMELINE@{DRM\_CAP\_SYNCOBJ\_TIMELINE}!drm.h@{drm.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{DRM\_CAP\_SYNCOBJ\_TIMELINE}{DRM\_CAP\_SYNCOBJ\_TIMELINE}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+C\+A\+P\+\_\+\+S\+Y\+N\+C\+O\+B\+J\+\_\+\+T\+I\+M\+E\+L\+I\+NE~0x14}

D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+C\+A\+P\+\_\+\+S\+Y\+N\+C\+O\+B\+J\+\_\+\+T\+I\+M\+E\+L\+I\+NE

If set to 1, the driver supports timeline operations on sync objects. See Documentation/gpu/drm-\/mm.\+rst, section \char`\"{}\+D\+R\+M Sync Objects\char`\"{}. \mbox{\Hypertarget{drm_8h_a4e56bf4a35b3bdac17a9d783133ca724}\label{drm_8h_a4e56bf4a35b3bdac17a9d783133ca724}} 
\index{drm.h@{drm.h}!DRM\_CAP\_TIMESTAMP\_MONOTONIC@{DRM\_CAP\_TIMESTAMP\_MONOTONIC}}
\index{DRM\_CAP\_TIMESTAMP\_MONOTONIC@{DRM\_CAP\_TIMESTAMP\_MONOTONIC}!drm.h@{drm.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{DRM\_CAP\_TIMESTAMP\_MONOTONIC}{DRM\_CAP\_TIMESTAMP\_MONOTONIC}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+C\+A\+P\+\_\+\+T\+I\+M\+E\+S\+T\+A\+M\+P\+\_\+\+M\+O\+N\+O\+T\+O\+N\+IC~0x6}

D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+C\+A\+P\+\_\+\+T\+I\+M\+E\+S\+T\+A\+M\+P\+\_\+\+M\+O\+N\+O\+T\+O\+N\+IC

If set to 0, the kernel will report timestamps with {\ttfamily C\+L\+O\+C\+K\+\_\+\+R\+E\+A\+L\+T\+I\+ME} in struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structdrm__event__vblank}{drm\+\_\+event\+\_\+vblank}}. If set to 1, the kernel will report timestamps with {\ttfamily C\+L\+O\+C\+K\+\_\+\+M\+O\+N\+O\+T\+O\+N\+IC}. See {\ttfamily clock\+\_\+gettime(2)} for the definition of these clocks.

Starting from kernel version 2.\+6.\+39, the default value for this capability is 1. Starting kernel version 4.\+15, this capability is always set to 1. \mbox{\Hypertarget{drm_8h_a6cbbb2c6f8e67378e72646d3c8611608}\label{drm_8h_a6cbbb2c6f8e67378e72646d3c8611608}} 
\index{drm.h@{drm.h}!DRM\_CAP\_VBLANK\_HIGH\_CRTC@{DRM\_CAP\_VBLANK\_HIGH\_CRTC}}
\index{DRM\_CAP\_VBLANK\_HIGH\_CRTC@{DRM\_CAP\_VBLANK\_HIGH\_CRTC}!drm.h@{drm.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{DRM\_CAP\_VBLANK\_HIGH\_CRTC}{DRM\_CAP\_VBLANK\_HIGH\_CRTC}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+C\+A\+P\+\_\+\+V\+B\+L\+A\+N\+K\+\_\+\+H\+I\+G\+H\+\_\+\+C\+R\+TC~0x2}

D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+C\+A\+P\+\_\+\+V\+B\+L\+A\+N\+K\+\_\+\+H\+I\+G\+H\+\_\+\+C\+R\+TC

If set to 1, the kernel supports specifying a \+:ref\+:{\ttfamily C\+R\+TC index$<$crtc\+\_\+index$>$} in the high bits of \&\mbox{\hyperlink{structdrm__wait__vblank__request_a0aad06062bfcb8fa6e919463fd3a0220}{drm\+\_\+wait\+\_\+vblank\+\_\+request.\+type}}.

Starting kernel version 2.\+6.\+39, this capability is always set to 1. \mbox{\Hypertarget{drm_8h_a44dbfa0df16d2c4fd013da0720a14862}\label{drm_8h_a44dbfa0df16d2c4fd013da0720a14862}} 
\index{drm.h@{drm.h}!DRM\_CLIENT\_CAP\_ASPECT\_RATIO@{DRM\_CLIENT\_CAP\_ASPECT\_RATIO}}
\index{DRM\_CLIENT\_CAP\_ASPECT\_RATIO@{DRM\_CLIENT\_CAP\_ASPECT\_RATIO}!drm.h@{drm.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{DRM\_CLIENT\_CAP\_ASPECT\_RATIO}{DRM\_CLIENT\_CAP\_ASPECT\_RATIO}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+C\+L\+I\+E\+N\+T\+\_\+\+C\+A\+P\+\_\+\+A\+S\+P\+E\+C\+T\+\_\+\+R\+A\+T\+IO~4}

D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+C\+L\+I\+E\+N\+T\+\_\+\+C\+A\+P\+\_\+\+A\+S\+P\+E\+C\+T\+\_\+\+R\+A\+T\+IO

If set to 1, the D\+RM core will provide aspect ratio information in modes. See {\ttfamily D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+M\+O\+D\+E\+\_\+\+F\+L\+A\+G\+\_\+\+P\+I\+C\+\_\+\+A\+R\+\_\+$\ast$}.

This capability is always supported for all drivers starting from kernel version 4.\+18. \mbox{\Hypertarget{drm_8h_a4cd133627d1aec40ed709cc2d926b36b}\label{drm_8h_a4cd133627d1aec40ed709cc2d926b36b}} 
\index{drm.h@{drm.h}!DRM\_CLIENT\_CAP\_ATOMIC@{DRM\_CLIENT\_CAP\_ATOMIC}}
\index{DRM\_CLIENT\_CAP\_ATOMIC@{DRM\_CLIENT\_CAP\_ATOMIC}!drm.h@{drm.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{DRM\_CLIENT\_CAP\_ATOMIC}{DRM\_CLIENT\_CAP\_ATOMIC}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+C\+L\+I\+E\+N\+T\+\_\+\+C\+A\+P\+\_\+\+A\+T\+O\+M\+IC~3}

D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+C\+L\+I\+E\+N\+T\+\_\+\+C\+A\+P\+\_\+\+A\+T\+O\+M\+IC

If set to 1, the D\+RM core will expose atomic properties to userspace. This implicitly enables \&D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+C\+L\+I\+E\+N\+T\+\_\+\+C\+A\+P\+\_\+\+U\+N\+I\+V\+E\+R\+S\+A\+L\+\_\+\+P\+L\+A\+N\+ES and \&D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+C\+L\+I\+E\+N\+T\+\_\+\+C\+A\+P\+\_\+\+A\+S\+P\+E\+C\+T\+\_\+\+R\+A\+T\+IO.

If the driver doesn\textquotesingle{}t support atomic mode-\/setting, enabling this capability will fail with -\/E\+O\+P\+N\+O\+T\+S\+U\+PP.

This capability has been introduced in kernel version 4.\+0. Starting from kernel version 4.\+2, this capability is always supported for atomic-\/capable drivers. \mbox{\Hypertarget{drm_8h_ad07352f5301c3cf3a8e20be4d3809487}\label{drm_8h_ad07352f5301c3cf3a8e20be4d3809487}} 
\index{drm.h@{drm.h}!DRM\_CLIENT\_CAP\_STEREO\_3D@{DRM\_CLIENT\_CAP\_STEREO\_3D}}
\index{DRM\_CLIENT\_CAP\_STEREO\_3D@{DRM\_CLIENT\_CAP\_STEREO\_3D}!drm.h@{drm.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{DRM\_CLIENT\_CAP\_STEREO\_3D}{DRM\_CLIENT\_CAP\_STEREO\_3D}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+C\+L\+I\+E\+N\+T\+\_\+\+C\+A\+P\+\_\+\+S\+T\+E\+R\+E\+O\+\_\+3D~1}

D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+C\+L\+I\+E\+N\+T\+\_\+\+C\+A\+P\+\_\+\+S\+T\+E\+R\+E\+O\+\_\+3D

If set to 1, the D\+RM core will expose the stereo 3D capabilities of the monitor by advertising the supported 3D layouts in the flags of struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structdrm__mode__modeinfo}{drm\+\_\+mode\+\_\+modeinfo}}. See {\ttfamily D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+M\+O\+D\+E\+\_\+\+F\+L\+A\+G\+\_\+3\+D\+\_\+$\ast$}.

This capability is always supported for all drivers starting from kernel version 3.\+13. \mbox{\Hypertarget{drm_8h_a442bbdbed0258e82a195fffde19c6bd5}\label{drm_8h_a442bbdbed0258e82a195fffde19c6bd5}} 
\index{drm.h@{drm.h}!DRM\_CLIENT\_CAP\_UNIVERSAL\_PLANES@{DRM\_CLIENT\_CAP\_UNIVERSAL\_PLANES}}
\index{DRM\_CLIENT\_CAP\_UNIVERSAL\_PLANES@{DRM\_CLIENT\_CAP\_UNIVERSAL\_PLANES}!drm.h@{drm.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{DRM\_CLIENT\_CAP\_UNIVERSAL\_PLANES}{DRM\_CLIENT\_CAP\_UNIVERSAL\_PLANES}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+C\+L\+I\+E\+N\+T\+\_\+\+C\+A\+P\+\_\+\+U\+N\+I\+V\+E\+R\+S\+A\+L\+\_\+\+P\+L\+A\+N\+ES~2}

D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+C\+L\+I\+E\+N\+T\+\_\+\+C\+A\+P\+\_\+\+U\+N\+I\+V\+E\+R\+S\+A\+L\+\_\+\+P\+L\+A\+N\+ES

If set to 1, the D\+RM core will expose all planes (overlay, primary, and cursor) to userspace.

This capability has been introduced in kernel version 3.\+15. Starting from kernel version 3.\+17, this capability is always supported for all drivers. \mbox{\Hypertarget{drm_8h_a9f389eb0537dcf9d2ab9926ed79e5885}\label{drm_8h_a9f389eb0537dcf9d2ab9926ed79e5885}} 
\index{drm.h@{drm.h}!DRM\_CLIENT\_CAP\_WRITEBACK\_CONNECTORS@{DRM\_CLIENT\_CAP\_WRITEBACK\_CONNECTORS}}
\index{DRM\_CLIENT\_CAP\_WRITEBACK\_CONNECTORS@{DRM\_CLIENT\_CAP\_WRITEBACK\_CONNECTORS}!drm.h@{drm.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{DRM\_CLIENT\_CAP\_WRITEBACK\_CONNECTORS}{DRM\_CLIENT\_CAP\_WRITEBACK\_CONNECTORS}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+C\+L\+I\+E\+N\+T\+\_\+\+C\+A\+P\+\_\+\+W\+R\+I\+T\+E\+B\+A\+C\+K\+\_\+\+C\+O\+N\+N\+E\+C\+T\+O\+RS~5}

D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+C\+L\+I\+E\+N\+T\+\_\+\+C\+A\+P\+\_\+\+W\+R\+I\+T\+E\+B\+A\+C\+K\+\_\+\+C\+O\+N\+N\+E\+C\+T\+O\+RS

If set to 1, the D\+RM core will expose special connectors to be used for writing back to memory the scene setup in the commit. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} client must enable \&D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+C\+L\+I\+E\+N\+T\+\_\+\+C\+A\+P\+\_\+\+A\+T\+O\+M\+IC first.

This capability is always supported for atomic-\/capable drivers starting from kernel version 4.\+19. \mbox{\Hypertarget{drm_8h_a50fe4beeba52b99aea2866ad82c68527}\label{drm_8h_a50fe4beeba52b99aea2866ad82c68527}}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+I\+O\+C\+T\+L\+\_\+\+M\+O\+D\+E\+\_\+\+R\+M\+FB~\mbox{\hyperlink{drm_8h_a4d87f2451bced4acf9ec73b614f13675}{D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+I\+O\+WR}}(0x\+AF, unsigned \mbox{\hyperlink{dcache_8h_ad65b480f8c8270356b45a9890f6499ae}{int}})}

D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+I\+O\+C\+T\+L\+\_\+\+M\+O\+D\+E\+\_\+\+R\+M\+FB -\/ Remove a framebuffer.

This removes a framebuffer previously added via A\+D\+D\+F\+B/\+A\+D\+D\+F\+B2. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} I\+O\+C\+TL argument is a framebuffer object ID.

Warning\+: removing a framebuffer currently in-\/use on an enabled plane will disable that plane. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} C\+R\+TC the plane is linked to may also be disabled (depending on driver capabilities). \mbox{\Hypertarget{drm_8h_addd3ef6c8f2a2d9883650b2c9870eb49}\label{drm_8h_addd3ef6c8f2a2d9883650b2c9870eb49}}
